<!-- This is where implementation and generic API methods for interacting with the 3rd part CS chat service go. -->
<script>
  (function() {
    /**
     * Returns the settings and config needed for the chat service.
     *
     * @return {object} Customer support chat service settings.
     */
    function getChatSettings() {
      var userInfo = (dataLayer && dataLayer.length && dataLayer[0]) || {}

      return {
        identify: {
          signature: ``,
          name: String(userInfo.userName),
          email: String(userInfo.userEmail || __USER__.email),
          "User ID": String(userInfo.userId),
          "Order Number": String(userInfo.orderNumber || __USER__.currentOrder),
          "Active Platinum": String(userInfo.platinum),
          "Active PLUS": String(userInfo.plus),
          "Logged In": String(userInfo.loggedIn),
        },
        config: {
          display: { style: 'manual' }
        }
      }
    }

    /**
     * Injects customer support chat service into frontend.
     */
    function injectChatScript() {
      var scriptNode = document.createElement('script')
      scriptNode.type = 'text/javascript'
      scriptNode.async = true
      scriptNode.src = 'https://beacon-v2.helpscout.net'

      var whereToAdd = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0]
      whereToAdd.parentNode.insertBefore(scriptNode, whereToAdd)
    }

    /**
     * Sets up the customer support chat service.
     */
    function setupCustomerSupportChat() {
      window.beaconSettings = getChatSettings()

      if (typeof window.Beacon !== 'function') {
        var beacon = function(t, n, a) {
            beacon.readyQueue.push({
                method: t,
                options: n,
                data: a
            })
        }

        beacon.readyQueue = []

        window.Beacon = beacon;

        if (document.readyState === "complete") {
          injectChatScript()
        } else if (window.attachEvent) {
          window.attachEvent('onload', injectChatScript)
        } else {
          window.addEventListener('load', injectChatScript, false)
        }

        // Destroys Beacon whenever a new link is clicked
        document.addEventListener('turbolinks:visit', function() {
          window.Beacon('destroy')
        })

        // Reinitializes Beacon when the page has loaded
        document.addEventListener('turbolinks:load', function() {
          window.Beacon('init', '063df7b1-e97a-4c44-a895-194169a2e13e')
          window.Beacon('config', window.beaconSettings.config)
          window.Beacon('identify', window.beaconSettings.identify)
        })

        // Adds Event tracking for Beacon Open/Close events
        window.Beacon('on', 'open', () => {
          $.track('event', 'HelpScout', 'Open', 'Beacon was Opened', undefined, true)
        })
        window.Beacon('on', 'close', () => {
          $.track('event', 'HelpScout', 'Close', 'Beacon was Closed', undefined, true)
        })
      }
    }

    /**
     * Sets up a generic API for interacting with the customer support chat service.
     */
    function setupExposedChatAPI() {
      window.CustomerSupport = {
        /**
         * Opens chat messages.
         * @see https://developer.helpscout.com/beacon-2/web/javascript-api/#beaconopenclose
         */
        openChat: function() {
          if (window.Beacon) {
            window.Beacon('open')
          }
        },
        /**
         * Closes chat messsages.
         * @see https://developer.helpscout.com/beacon-2/web/javascript-api/#beaconopenclose
         */
        closeChat: function() {
          if (window.Beacon) {
            window.Beacon('close')
          }
        },
        /**
         * Displays the chat bubble.
         * @see https://developer.helpscout.com/beacon-2/web/javascript-api/#display-options
         */
        showChatBubble: function() {
          if (window.Beacon) {
            window.Beacon('config', { display: { style: 'icon', iconImage: 'message' } } )
          }
        },
        /**
         * Hides the chat bubble.
         * @see https://developer.helpscout.com/beacon-2/web/javascript-api/#display-options
         */
        hideChatBubble: function() {
          if (window.Beacon) {
            window.Beacon('config', window.beaconSettings.config )
          }
        },
      }
    }

    // We only want to show the CS chat stuff on everything but test
      setupCustomerSupportChat()

    // Expose a generic API for us to interact with the chat service.
    setupExposedChatAPI()
  })()
</script>
